# Agents · O · part 2

1,021 entries, okra-orchestrator to opencode-native-permission. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **okra-orchestrator**: OKRA loop steering agent. Use for check-in rounds, admissibility screening before dispatching work, reading run-store state, deciding the next move, appending check-ins/flags/metric reads, and composing worker prompt packets. It never executes product work itself and never edits the frame — it steers inside it. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-orchestrator.md))
- **okra-validator**: Deterministic verification agent for OKRA work. Use after any change to the skill, checkers, evals, runner, or run store — or whenever a claim ("anti-goal held", "checker passes", "artifact complete", "done") needs to become an executable audit trace instead of an assertion. It replays evidence mechanically and reports accepted/rejected decisions with hashes; it does not judge prose quality and does not fix anything. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-validator.md))
- **okra-writer**: Deep-work execution agent for this repository — progresses the actual work: editing the reverse-tornado-okr skill, references, contracts, blindbox checkers, golden corpora, cases, fixtures, and the runner. Use for any DKR probe or PKR/task dispatched by the orchestrator, or any non-trivial implementation the user asks for. It executes within its packet scope, hands back on unknowns, and never validates its own claims. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-writer.md))
- **okta-auth-specialist**: Okta/Auth0 OIDC specialist implementing the BFF pattern — auth-code + PKCE, server-side sessions, token exchange, step-up MFA, and downstream authorization policies. Use for any login, session, scope, claims, or authorization work. ([MeridianLZ/copilot-plugins](https://github.com/MeridianLZ/copilot-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fintech-backend/agents/okta-auth-specialist.md) · 2 copies)
- **oktopost-analytics-interpreter**: Analytics interpreter subagent for social media performance analysis and actionable insights ([Oktopost/oktopost-claude](https://github.com/Oktopost/oktopost-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/analytics-interpreter.md))
- **oktopost-content-strategist**: Content strategist subagent for B2B social content ideation and calendar planning ([Oktopost/oktopost-claude](https://github.com/Oktopost/oktopost-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/content-strategist.md))
- **okuyucu**: Answers a code question the user asked while the main agent was busy, by reading the repository. Read-only. Runs on Haiku by decision 035; the haiku guard in ust.js does not cover this file. ([Teknesyum/Teknesyum-Core](https://github.com/Teknesyum/Teknesyum-Core/blob/HEAD/core/agents/okuyucu.md) · ★2)
- **okuyucu**: Salt-okuma analiz alt ajanı (sonnet). Transcript karşılaştırma, resmi doküman araştırması, kör puan. Dosya yazmaz/değiştirmez; yalnız istenen özeti kanıt satırlarıyla döner. ([Omer-F-Ylmz/omer-skills](https://github.com/Omer-F-Ylmz/omer-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okuyucu.md) · ★1)
- **OKX Trader**: Трейдер-оператор OKX через официальный okx CLI и скиллы из .agents/skills: ордера (спот, своп, фьючерсы), TP/SL, grid- и DCA-боты, баланс, позиции, котировки, funding, сентимент, smart money. - **ollama**: Ollama (`ollama` CLI) runs local models on the user's machine. It is a gateway transport in the family sense — one CLI, many architectures — but unlike OpenCode every call stays on localhost. No provider credentials, no external rate limits, no network round-trip. Trade-off: the user must have `ollama serve` running and must have pulled the chosen model beforehand. ([r-uben/disputatio](https://github.com/r-uben/disputatio/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ollama.md) · ★1)

- **ollama-admin**: Use this agent when the user asks to manage or inspect Ollama models. Typical triggers include "what models do I have", "check what models are running", "pull model X", "download a model", "show model details for...", "how big is model Y", "what models are available", "get model info", or any Ollama model lifecycle operation that does NOT involve deleting, copying, or creating models. Also triggers for monitoring questions like "is Ollama running", "Ollama status", or "how much VRAM are my models using". See "When to invoke" in the agent body for detailed trigger scenarios. Trigger on: triage the open items, what is actually still open, OI backlog, dedupe the tracker, are these still relevant, open items review, close out the tracker. Reports only — never edits the tracker, never marks anything done, never allocates an OI id. N tasks — even eight — still cost ONE Bash call: the script fans them out itself with bounded concurrency. Each model answer is written to its own file, never printed; this agent's context grows by one manifest-path line, whatever N is.
